# Formats and Conversion Paths

Generated by `sublime paths --markdown`. Do not edit by hand. CI fails if this file is stale.

## Map

One graph per category and one for the crossings between them. A line joins two formats with a direct converter between them, in either direction. The grid under each graph gives every pair (rows from, columns to): <img src="map/lossless.svg?v=2" width="18" alt="lossless"> lossless, <img src="map/conditional.svg?v=2" width="18" alt="conditional"> conditional, <img src="map/lossy.svg?v=2" width="18" alt="lossy"> lossy, with the number of steps (1 is a direct converter), blank for no path. The Paths table below has each path and what it loses.

### Between categories

A format's converter into another category opens every path that category already has, so the other side is drawn as the category itself.

```mermaid
graph LR
  xlsx --- document{{document}}
  numbers --- document{{document}}
  json --- document{{document}}
  csv --- document{{document}}
  tsv --- document{{document}}
  pdf --- image{{image}}
```

## Converters

| Name | From | To | Tier | Fidelity | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| csv-to-json | csv | json | native | lossless |  |
| json-to-csv | json | csv | native | conditional | nested values are written as JSON text, non-string scalars are written as their JSON text, null becomes an empty cell, and objects with differing key sets get empty cells for missing keys |
| markdown-to-html | markdown | html | native | lossless |  |
| markdown-to-text | markdown | text | native | lossy | markup, raw HTML, and image sources are dropped |
| markdown-to-json | markdown | markdown-json | native | lossless |  |
| markdown-json-to-markdown | markdown-json | markdown | native | lossless |  |
| pages-to-json | pages | pages-json | native | lossless |  |
| json-to-pages | pages-json | pages | native | lossless |  |
| pages-to-docx | pages | docx | native | conditional | text, named styles, lists (with their indents and numbering), links, footnotes, comments and reply threads (author, date, text, and range), tracked insertions and deletions, headers and footers, section columns and column breaks, tab stops, and paragraph borders carry over; tables keep merges, fills, borders, and cell alignment, including tables placed on the page; shapes, lines, and text boxes keep their outline, fill, stroke, and text wrap, moving with their paragraph when anchored in the text or sitting in its line, behind the text where Pages draws them there, with their arrowheads; empty paragraphs keep the size of their mark; charts become native Word charts with their data; drawings on page templates return to their header or footer; the page colour becomes the Word page colour; images keep their place |
| pages-to-markdown | pages | markdown | native | lossy | page layout, headers and footers, fonts, sizes, and colors are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| pages-to-html | pages | html | native | lossy | page layout, headers and footers, fonts, sizes, and colors are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| pages-to-text | pages | text | native | lossy | formatting, images, page layout, headers and footers are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| docx-to-markdown | docx | markdown | native | lossy | page layout, headers and footers, fonts, sizes, and colors are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| docx-to-html | docx | html | native | lossy | page layout, headers and footers, fonts, sizes, and colors are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| docx-to-text | docx | text | native | lossy | formatting, images, page layout, headers and footers are dropped; text boxes follow the body |
| markdown-to-docx | markdown | docx | native | conditional | lossless for headings, paragraphs, lists, quotes, code, tables, links, footnotes, and images given as data URIs; raw HTML is dropped, other images become links, and loose lists come out tight |
| markdown-to-pages | markdown | pages | native | lossy | paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, and tables reach Pages with its named styles, bold and italic runs reuse its character styles, and links become clickable hyperlinks; images are not yet written |
| text-to-pages | text | pages | native | lossy | paragraphs reach Pages as body text; text carries no other structure |
| html-to-pages | html | pages | native | lossy | paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, and tables reach Pages with its named styles, bold and italic runs reuse its character styles, and links become clickable hyperlinks; images are not yet written |
| docx-to-pages | docx | pages | native | lossy | paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |
| html-to-markdown | html | markdown | native | lossy | scripts, styles, forms, and layout are dropped; elements without a Markdown form keep their text |
| html-to-text | html | text | native | lossy | markup, scripts, styles, and image sources are dropped |
| html-to-docx | html | docx | native | conditional | lossless for headings, paragraphs, lists, quotes, code, tables, links, footnotes, and images given as data URIs; scripts, styles, forms, and layout are dropped, and other images become links |
| text-to-markdown | text | markdown | native | conditional | paragraphs are runs of lines separated by blank lines; text that looks like Markdown is escaped so it reads as written |
| text-to-html | text | html | native | conditional | paragraphs are runs of lines separated by blank lines; the text itself is kept exactly |
| text-to-docx | text | docx | native | conditional | paragraphs are runs of lines separated by blank lines; line breaks inside a paragraph become spaces |
| pdf-to-text | pdf | text | native | lossy | fonts, layout, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| pdf-to-markdown | pdf | markdown | native | lossy | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| pdf-to-html | pdf | html | native | lossy | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |

| rtf-to-docx | rtf | docx | native | lossy | paragraphs, named styles, run and paragraph formatting, lists, tables (merged cells, borders, shading, nesting), sections with page setup, columns, headers, and footers, links, page-number fields, footnotes, comments, tracked changes, pictures (PNG, JPEG, EMF, WMF, bitmaps), text boxes, and the page colour carry over; other fields keep their shown text, drawn shapes without text, OLE objects (their picture is kept), and text in double-byte code pages without Unicode escapes are not carried |
| rtf-to-pages | rtf | pages | native | lossy | paragraphs, named styles, run and paragraph formatting, lists, tables (merged cells, borders, shading, nesting), sections with page setup, columns, headers, and footers, links, page-number fields, footnotes, comments, tracked changes, pictures (PNG, JPEG, EMF, WMF, bitmaps), text boxes, and the page colour carry over; other fields keep their shown text, drawn shapes without text, OLE objects (their picture is kept), and text in double-byte code pages without Unicode escapes are not carried |
| rtf-to-markdown | rtf | markdown | native | lossy | headings, paragraphs, emphasis, lists, tables, links, footnotes, and images by name carry over; fonts, sizes, colours, alignment, page setup, headers and footers, comments, and tracked changes (insertions kept, deletions dropped) do not |
| rtf-to-html | rtf | html | native | lossy | headings, paragraphs, emphasis, lists, tables, links, footnotes, and images by name carry over; fonts, sizes, colours, alignment, page setup, headers and footers, comments, and tracked changes (insertions kept, deletions dropped) do not |

| docx-to-rtf | docx | rtf | native | lossy | paragraphs, named styles, run and paragraph formatting, lists (restarts and start numbers kept), tables (merged cells, borders, shading, nesting), sections with page setup, columns, headers, and footers, links, page-number fields, footnotes, comments with their replies, tracked changes, pictures (PNG, JPEG, EMF, WMF, BMP), text boxes, and the page colour carry over; charts, equations (their text is kept), and pictures in other formats do not |
| pages-to-rtf | pages | rtf | native | lossy | paragraphs, named styles, run and paragraph formatting, lists, tables, sections, headers and footers, links, footnotes, comments, tracked changes, pictures, text boxes, and the page colour carry over; charts and pictures in formats RTF cannot hold do not |
| markdown-to-rtf | markdown | rtf | native | lossy | headings, paragraphs, emphasis, lists, tables, links, footnotes, and code carry over as RTF formatting; images are kept only when the source holds their bytes |

| text-to-rtf | text | rtf | native | lossy | headings, paragraphs, emphasis, lists, tables, links, footnotes, and code carry over as RTF formatting; images are kept only when the source holds their bytes |

| xlsx-to-csv | xlsx | csv | native | conditional | each sheet its own file (or the one --sheet picks); numbers as stored, dates as ISO 8601, booleans as TRUE and FALSE, formulas as their last value; formatting is dropped |
| xlsx-to-tsv | xlsx | tsv | native | conditional | each sheet its own file (or the one --sheet picks); numbers as stored, dates as ISO 8601, booleans as TRUE and FALSE, formulas as their last value; formatting is dropped |
| xlsx-to-json | xlsx | json | native | conditional | one sheet as an array of objects keyed by its header row, several as an object of such arrays keyed by sheet name (or the one --sheet picks); every value as text: numbers as stored, dates as ISO 8601, booleans as TRUE and FALSE, formulas as their last value; formatting is dropped |
| xlsx-to-markdown | xlsx | markdown | native | conditional | each sheet a table under a heading of its name (one sheet, a table alone), the first row as the header; every value as text, rows padded or cut to the header's width, line breaks inside cells as spaces; formatting is dropped |
| numbers-to-csv | numbers | csv | native | conditional | each table its own file (or the one --sheet picks), named after its sheet; cells as Numbers shows them, merged-over cells empty; formatting, formulas, charts, and pictures are dropped |

| numbers-to-markdown | numbers | markdown | native | conditional | each table under a heading of its name (one table, the table alone), the first row as the header; cells as Numbers shows them, line breaks inside cells as spaces; formatting, formulas, charts, and pictures are dropped |
| numbers-to-xlsx | numbers | xlsx | native | conditional | each table a worksheet (or the one --sheet picks); numbers, dates, and booleans as cells of their type, everything else as text; merged cells kept; formatting, formulas, charts, and pictures are dropped |
| csv-to-numbers | csv | numbers | native | conditional | a sheet per worksheet (delimited rows one sheet, JSON a sheet per table), each one table with its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; a workbook's number and date formats where Numbers has the same (decimals, currency, percent, scientific, fraction, date patterns) and its merged cells; Numbers' default table style otherwise |
| tsv-to-numbers | tsv | numbers | native | conditional | a sheet per worksheet (delimited rows one sheet, JSON a sheet per table), each one table with its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; a workbook's number and date formats where Numbers has the same (decimals, currency, percent, scientific, fraction, date patterns) and its merged cells; Numbers' default table style otherwise |
| xlsx-to-numbers | xlsx | numbers | native | conditional | a sheet per worksheet (delimited rows one sheet, JSON a sheet per table), each one table with its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; a workbook's number and date formats where Numbers has the same (decimals, currency, percent, scientific, fraction, date patterns) and its merged cells; Numbers' default table style otherwise |
| json-to-numbers | json | numbers | native | conditional | a sheet per worksheet (delimited rows one sheet, JSON a sheet per table), each one table with its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; a workbook's number and date formats where Numbers has the same (decimals, currency, percent, scientific, fraction, date patterns) and its merged cells; Numbers' default table style otherwise |
| markdown-to-numbers | markdown | numbers | native | lossy | the document's tables as tables of one sheet, each named after the heading before it, its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; everything but the tables is dropped |
| markdown-to-xlsx | markdown | xlsx | native | lossy | each of the document's tables a sheet, named after the heading before it, inline formatting flattened to text, decimals as numbers, ISO dates as dates, and TRUE and FALSE as booleans; everything else in the document is dropped |
| json-to-xlsx | json | xlsx | native | conditional | an array of objects as one sheet, an object of such arrays as a sheet per member; keys become the header row, nested values JSON text, decimals numbers, ISO date strings dates (JSON true and false stay text: a workbook boolean reads back as TRUE), and objects with differing key sets get empty cells for missing keys |

## Paths

| From | To | Fidelity | Path | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| bmp | csv | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown -> markdown-to-csv | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; each of the document's tables its own file, named after the heading before it, inline formatting flattened to text; everything else in the document is dropped |
| bmp | docx | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-docx | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| bmp | html | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-html | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| bmp | ico | conditional | bmp-to-ico | the image is the icon's largest size (scaled down to 256 pixels if larger), with the standard smaller sizes added, scaled by area averaging |
| bmp | jpeg | lossy | bmp-to-jpeg | JPEG is lossy: the image is re-encoded at the quality given (85 by default, 4:2:0 chroma below 90) and alpha is flattened onto white |
| bmp | json | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown -> markdown-tables-to-json | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; the document's tables only: one as an array of objects keyed by its header, several as an object of such arrays keyed by the heading before each; inline formatting is flattened to text and everything else in the document is dropped |
| bmp | jsonl | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown -> markdown-to-csv -> csv-to-jsonl | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; each of the document's tables its own file, named after the heading before it, inline formatting flattened to text; everything else in the document is dropped |
| bmp | markdown | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| bmp | markdown-json | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown -> markdown-to-json | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read |
| bmp | numbers | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-markdown -> markdown-to-numbers | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; the document's tables as tables of one sheet, each named after the heading before it, its first row the header; plain decimals become numbers, ISO 8601 dates and dates with times dates, TRUE and FALSE booleans, everything else text; everything but the tables is dropped |
| bmp | pages | lossy | bmp-to-pdf -> pdf-to-docx -> docx-to-pages | headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

| docx | pages | lossy | docx-to-pages | paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

| text | webp | conditional | text-to-pdf -> pdf-to-webp | set on Letter pages in Helvetica and Courier, with fonts installed on this machine (subset and embedded) for other scripts; images set as blocks at their size, scaled to the page (JPEG embedded unchanged), and shown by their alt text when not found (web addresses are not fetched), not readable (GIF), or in a table; raw HTML is dropped, and characters no installed font covers become ?; the chosen page's largest image is read (--page, the first by default); a page's vector content is not rendered, and other pages and images are dropped |
| text | xlsx | lossy | text-to-markdown -> markdown-to-xlsx | paragraphs are runs of lines separated by blank lines; text that looks like Markdown is escaped so it reads as written; each of the document's tables a sheet, named after the heading before it, inline formatting flattened to text, decimals as numbers, ISO dates as dates, and TRUE and FALSE as booleans; everything else in the document is dropped |
| text | xml | lossy | text-to-markdown -> markdown-tables-to-json -> json-to-xml | paragraphs are runs of lines separated by blank lines; text that looks like Markdown is escaped so it reads as written; the document's tables only: one as an array of objects keyed by its header, several as an object of such arrays keyed by the heading before each; inline formatting is flattened to text and everything else in the document is dropped; @-keys become attributes, #text the text, arrays repeated elements; the root object must have one member, otherwise it is wrapped in &lt;root&gt;; numbers, booleans, and nulls become text |

| tga | pages | lossy | tga-to-pdf -> pdf-to-docx -> docx-to-pages | the ID field and any TGA 2.0 extension area (thumbnail, author, dates) are dropped; headings are read from type size and weight and list items from their markers; fonts, layout, tables, images, and drawings are dropped; reading order is the order the page draws its text in; text in pages that are pictures (scans) is not read; paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; any number of tables keep merged cells, shading, borders, cell margins, vertical alignment, and per-run formatting, with their lists as Pages lists (numbered ones keeping their running count across cells); nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |

paragraphs, headings, bulleted and numbered lists, page size and margins, section columns (equal or of their own widths), page, column, and line breaks, and paragraph alignment, spacing, and indents reach Pages with its named styles; a run's bold, italic, colour, size, font (theme fonts included), underline, strikethrough, super- and subscript, caps, highlight, and raised or lowered text carry over (hidden text stays hidden); horizontal rules, page breaks before paragraphs, page numbering restarts, and legacy (VML) pictures and embedded objects' previews come through; nested tables flatten to tab-separated lines; headers and footers (first-page and odd/even variants, live page numbers) are native; links become clickable hyperlinks; footnotes and endnotes become Pages footnotes; comments and their reply threads keep their author, date, text, and range (overlapping ones too, and those on headers or cells are kept on the table or the body's start); tracked insertions and deletions stay tracked changes, with their authors and dates; inline images are placed with their bytes and size; shapes and text boxes (each one of a group) are drawn in place from Word's common presets with their fill and outline (those in the text line stay in it, those placed from a paragraph move with the text, and each keeps its text wrap, drawings behind the text stay behind it, arrowheads are drawn, and see-through fills are blended over the page); empty paragraphs keep the height of their paragraph mark; a document without headers or footers turns them off; drawings in headers and footers repeat on the pages of their variant; placement by page share, alignment, and relative size is honoured; the page colour fills the pages; framed paragraphs (page numbers beside a footer's text, positioned frames in the body) keep their place; pictures offered as PDF or metafile with a PNG fallback use the PNG; consecutive and trailing page breaks keep their pages; form checkboxes become box characters; charts become a table of their data and line arrowheads are not drawn |
